idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Tile shape of the x-y plane processed by one thread block.\nstatic constexpr int BX = 32;\nstatic constexpr int BY = 8;\n\n// Both sweeps of the algorithm are 7-point stencils with clamped boundaries and\n// only differ in the per-cell arithmetic, so they share one templated kernel.\n// The kernel walks a chunk of z-planes: the x/y neighbours of the current plane\n// live in shared memory while the z neighbours are kept in registers and rolled\n// forward, so every cell is loaded from global memory essentially once.\ntemplate <bool CHEM_POT>\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) chStencilKernel(\n    const double* __restrict__ in,    // c for the chemical potential, mu for the update\n    const double* __restrict__ cold,  // only used by the update sweep\n    double* __restrict__ out,\n    const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,\n    const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n    const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,\n    const double dtD, const int zChunk) {\n    __shared__ double s[BY + 2][BX + 2];\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BX + tx;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BY + ty;\n\n    // Threads outside the domain still participate in the loads (clamped) so\n    // that the halo entries they produce for their neighbours stay valid.\n    const int cx = (x < nx) ? x : nx - 1;\n    const int cy = (y < ny) ? y : ny - 1;\n    const bool active = (x < nx) && (y < ny);\n\n    const int xn = (cx > 0) ? cx - 1 : 0;\n    const int xp = (cx < nx - 1) ? cx + 1 : nx - 1;\n    const int yn = (cy > 0) ? cy - 1 : 0;\n    const int yp = (cy < ny - 1) ? cy + 1 : ny - 1;\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * static_cast<size_t>(ny);\n    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(cy) * nx;\n\n    const int z0 = blockIdx.z * zChunk;\n    const int z1 = min(z0 + zChunk, nz);\n    if (z0 >= z1) return;\n\n    const double dx2 = dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = dz * dz;\n\n    double prev = in[static_cast<size_t>((z0 > 0) ? z0 - 1 : 0) * plane + row + cx];\n    double cur = in[static_cast<size_t>(z0) * plane + row + cx];\n\n    for (int z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n        const size_t zoff = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane;\n        const double next = in[static_cast<size_t>((z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : nz - 1) * plane + row + cx];\n\n        s[ty + 1][tx + 1] = cur;\n        if (tx == 0) s[ty + 1][0] = in[zoff + row + xn];\n        if (tx == BX - 1) s[ty + 1][BX + 1] = in[zoff + row + xp];\n        if (ty == 0) s[0][tx + 1] = in[zoff + static_cast<size_t>(yn) * nx + cx];\n        if (ty == BY - 1) s[BY + 1][tx + 1] = in[zoff + static_cast<size_t>(yp) * nx + cx];\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (active) {\n            const double cxx = (s[ty + 1][tx + 2] + s[ty + 1][tx] - 2.0 * cur) / dx2;\n            const double cyy = (s[ty + 2][tx + 1] + s[ty][tx + 1] - 2.0 * cur) / dy2;\n            const double czz = (next + prev - 2.0 * cur) / dz2;\n            const double lap = cxx + cyy + czz;\n\n            const size_t idx = zoff + row + cx;\n            if (CHEM_POT) {\n                out[idx] = 4.5 * ((cur + 1.0) * e_AA + (cur - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cur * e_AB) + 3.0 * cur + cur * cur * cur - gamma * lap;\n            } else {\n                out[idx] = cold[idx] + dtD * lap;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        prev = cur;\n        cur = next;\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 64;\n    size_t ny = 0;\n    size_t nz = 0;\n    int iterations = 20;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n    const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,\n    const double dtD, const int zChunk) {\n    __shared__ double * dz;\n\n    double prev = in[static_cast<size_t>((z0 > 0) ? z0 - 1 : 0) * plane + row + cx];\n    double cur = in[static_cast<size_t>(z0) * plane + row + cx];\n\n    for (int z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n        const size_t zoff = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane;\n        const double next = in[static_cast<size_t>((z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : nz - 1) * plane + row + cx];\n\n        s[ty + 1][tx + 1] = cur;\n + cx];\n        if (ty == BY - 1) s[BY + 1][tx + 1] = in[zoff + static_cast<size_t>(yp) * nx + cx];\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (active) {\n            const double cxx = (s[ty + 1][tx + 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] kernel.\n// The kernel walks a chunk of z-planes: the x/y neighbours of the current plane\n// live in shared memory while the z neighbours are kept in registers and rolled\n// forward, so every cell potential, mu for the update\n    const double* __restrict__ cold,  // only used by the update sweep\n    double* __restrict__ out,\n    const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,\n    const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n    const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,\n    const double dtD, const int zChunk) {\n    __shared__ double s[BY + 2][BX + 2];\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BX + tx;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BY + ty;\n\n    // Threads outside the domain still participate in the loads (clamped) so\n    // that the halo entries they produce for their neighbours stay valid.\n    const int cx = (x < nx) ? x : nx - 1;\n    const int cy = (y < ny) ? y : ny - 1;\n    const bool active = (x < nx) && (y < ny);\n\n    const int xn = (cx > 0) ? cx - 1 : 0;\n    const int xp = (cx < nx - 1) ? cx + 1 : nx - 1;\n    const int yn = (cy > 0) ? cy - 1 : 0;\n    const int yp = (cy < ny - 1) ? cy + 1 : ny - 1;\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * static_cast<size_t>(ny);\n    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(cy) * nx;\n\n    const int z0 = blockIdx.z * zChunk;\n    const int z1 = min(z0 + zChunk, nz);\n    if (z0 >= z1) return;\n\n    const double dx2 = dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = dz * dz;\n\n    double prev = in[static_cast<size_t>((z0 > 0) ? z0 - 1 : 0) * plane + row + cx];\n    double cur = in[static_cast<size_t>(z0) * plane + row + cx];\n\n    for (int z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n        const size_t zoff = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane;\n        const double next = in[static_cast<size_t>((z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : nz - 1) * plane + row + cx];\n\n        s[ty + 1][tx + 1] = cur;\n        if (tx == 0) s[ty + 1][0] = in[zoff + row + xn];\n        if (tx == BX - 1) s[ty + 1][BX + 1] = in[zoff + row + xp];\n        if (ty == 0) s[0][tx + 1] = in[zoff + static_cast<size_t>(yn) * nx + cx];\n        if (ty == BY - 1) s[BY + 1][tx + 1] = in[zoff + static_cast<size_t>(yp) * nx + cx];\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (active) {\n            const double cxx = (s[ty + 1][tx + 2] + s[ty + 1][tx] - 2.0 * cur) / dx2;\n            const double cyy = (s[ty + 2][tx + 1] + s[ty][tx + 1] - 2.0 * cur) / dy2;\n            const double czz = (next + prev - 2.0 * cur) / dz2;\n            const double lap = cxx + cyy + czz;\n\n            const size_t idx = zoff + row + cx;\n            if (CHEM_POT) {\n                out[idx] = 4.5 * ((cur + 1.0) * e_AA + (cur - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cur * e_AB) + 3.0 * cur + cur * cur * cur - gamma * lap;\n            } else {\n                out[idx] = cold[idx] + dtD * lap;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        prev = cur;\n        cur = next;\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 64;\n    size_t ny = 0;\n    size_t nz = 0;\n    int iterations = 20;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    printf(\"Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n    printf(\"Time steps: %d\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Physical parameters\n    const double dx = 1.0;\n    const double dy = 1.0;\n    const double dz = 1.0;\n    const double dt = 0.01;\n    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double gamma = 0.5;\n    const double D = 1.0;\n\n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    // Allocate arrays\n    std::vector<double> cold(gridSize);\n\n    // Initialize concentration field\n    printf(\"Initializing concentration field...\\n\");\n    initializeConcentration(cold, nx, ny, nz);\n\n    // Set up the GPU\n    int device = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(device));\n    cudaDeviceProp prop;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&prop, device));\n    printf(\"CUDA device: %s (%d SMs)\\n\", prop.name, prop.multiProcessorCount);\n\n    double *d_cold = nullptr, *d_cnew = nullptr, *d_mu = nullptr;\n    const size_t bytes = gridSize * sizeof(double);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_cold, bytes));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_cnew, bytes));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_mu, bytes));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_cold, cold.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    const int inx = static_cast<int>(nx);\n    const int iny = static_cast<int>(ny);\n    const int inz = static_cast<int>(nz);\n\n    // Split the z direction into as many chunks as needed to keep every SM busy\n    // while still letting each block amortize its loads over a long z sweep.\n    const int blocksXY = ((inx + BX - 1) / BX) * ((iny + BY - 1) / BY);\n    const int targetBlocks = prop.multiProcessorCount * 8;\n    int zBlocks = (targetBlocks + blocksXY - 1) / blocksXY;\n    zBlocks = std::min(std::max(zBlocks, 1), inz);\n    const int zChunk = (inz + zBlocks - 1) / zChunk_guard(zBlocks);\n\n    const dim3 block(BX, BY, 1);\n    const dim3 grid((inx + BX - 1) / BX, (iny + BY - 1) / BY, (inz + zChunk - 1) / zChunk);\n\n    // Run simulation\n    printf(\"Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\\n\");\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {\n        // Compute chemical potential\n        chStencilKernel<true><<<grid, block>>>(d_cold, nullptr, d_mu, inx, iny, inz,\n                                               dx, dy, dz, gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB, dt * D, zChunk);\n\n        // Update concentration\n        chStencilKernel<false><<<grid, block>>>(d_mu, d_cold, d_cnew, inx, iny, inz,\n                                                dx, dy, dz, gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB, dt * D, duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate performance\n    double cellUpdates = (double)gridSize * iterations;\n    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(cold.data(), d_cold, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_cold));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_cnew));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_mu));\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(cold, \"Concentration\");\n    }\n\n \\\n    do {                                                                                     \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                      \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                            \\\n            printf(\"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_), __FILE__, __LINE__); \\\n            exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                                               \\\n        }                                                                                     \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// 3D index calculation\n__host__ __device__ inline size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Tile shape of the x-y plane processed by one thread block.\nstatic constexpr int BX = 32;\nstatic constexpr int BY = 8;\n\n// Both sweeps of the algorithm are 7-point stencils with clamped boundaries and\n// only differ in the per-cell arithmetic, so they share one templated kernel.\n// The kernel walks a chunk of z-planes: the x/y neighbours of the current plane\n// live in shared memory while the z neighbours are kept in registers and rolled\n// forward, so every cell is loaded from global memory essentially once.\ntemplate <bool CHEM_POT>\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) chStencilKernel(\n    const double* __restrict__ in,    // c for the chemical potential, mu for the update\n    const double* __restrict__ cold,  // only used by the update sweep\n    double* __restrict__ out,\n    const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,\n    const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n    const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,\n    const double dtD, const int zChunk) {\n    __shared__ double s[BY + 2][BX + 2];\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BX + tx;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BY + ty;\n\n    // Threads outside the domain still participate in the loads (clamped) so\n    // that the halo entries they produce for their neighbours stay valid.\n    const int cx = (x < nx) ? x : nx - 1;\n    const int cy = (y < ny) ? y : ny - 1;\n    const bool active = (x < nx) && (y < ny);\n\n    const int xn = (cx > 0) ? cx - 1 : 0;\n    const int xp = (cx < nx - 1) ? cx + 1 : nx - 1;\n    const int yn = (cy > 0) ? cy - 1 : 0;\n    const int yp = (cy < ny - 1) ? cy + 1 : ny - 1;\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * static_cast<size_t>(ny);\n    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(cy) * nx;\n\n    const int z0 = blockIdx.z * zChunk;\n    const int z1 = min(z0 + zChunk, nz);\n    if (z0 >= z1) return;\n\n    const double dx2 = dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = dz * dz;\n\n    double prev = in[static_cast<size_t>((z0 > 0) ? z0 - 1 : 0) * plane + row + cx];\n    double cur = in[static_cast<size_t>(z0) * plane + row + cx];\n\n    for (int z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n        const size_t zoff = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane;\n        const double next = in[static_cast<size_t>((z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : nz - 1) * plane + row + cx];\n\n        s[ty + 1][tx + 1] = cur;\n        if (tx == 0) s[ty + 1][0] = in[zoff + row + xn];\n        if (tx == BX - 1) s[ty + 1][BX + 1] = in[zoff + row + xp];\n        if (ty == 0) s[0][tx + 1] = in[zoff + static_cast<size_t>(yn) * nx + cx];\n        if (ty == BY - 1) s[BY + 1][tx + 1] = in[zoff + static_cast<size_t>(yp) * nx + cx];\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (active) {\n            const double cxx = (s[ty + 1][tx + 2] + s[ty + 1][tx] - 2.0 * cur) / dx2;\n            const double cyy = (s[ty + 2][tx + 1] + s[ty][tx + 1] - 2.0 * cur) / dy2;\n            const double czz = (next + prev - 2.0 * cur) / dz2;\n            const double lap = cxx + cyy + czz;\n\n            const size_t idx = zoff + row + cx;\n            if (CHEM_POT) {\n                out[idx] = 4.5 * ((cur + 1.0) * e_AA + (cur - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cur * e_AB) + 3.0 * cur + cur * cur * cur - gamma * lap;\n            } else {\n                out[idx] = cold[idx] + dtD * lap;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        prev = cur;\n        cur = next;\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\",   printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable that the halo entries they produce for their neighbours stay valid.\n    const int cx = (x < nx) ? x : nx - 1;\n    const int cy = (y < ny) ? y : ny - 1;\n    const bool active = (x < nx) && (y < ny);\n\n    const int xn = (cx > 0) ? cx - 1 : 0;\n    const int xp = (cx < nx - 1) ? cx + 1 : nx - 1;\n    const int yn = (cy > 0) ? cy - 1 : 0;\n    const int yp = (cy < ny - 1) ? cy + 1 : ny -    if (!(v > 0.0) || std::isinf(v)) return false;\n    const double r = 1.0 / v;\n    return std::isnormal(r) && r * v == 1.0 && std::fabs(std::frexp(r, new int)) == 0.5;\n}\n\n// Initialize <cuda_runtime.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                     \\\n    do {                                                                                     \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                      \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                            \\\n            printf(\"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_), __FILE__, __LINE__); \\\n            exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                                               \\\n        }                                                                                     \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// 3D index calculation\n__host__ __device__ inline size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Tile shape of the x-y plane processed by one thread block.\nstatic constexpr int BX = 32;\nstatic constexpr int BY = 8;\n\n// Both sweeps of the algorithm are 7-point stencils with clamped boundaries and\n// only differ in the per-cell arithmetic, so they share one templated kernel.\n// The kernel walks a chunk of z-planes: the x/y neighbours of the current plane\n// live in shared memory while the z neighbours are kept in registers and rolled\n// forward, so every cell is loaded from global memory essentially once.\n// RECIP selects multiplication by the precomputed reciprocal of the squared grid\n// spacing instead of a division. It is only enabled when 1/d^2 is exactly\n// representable (see reciprocalIsExact), in which case both forms are bit-identical\n// but the multiplication is far cheaper in double precision.\ntemplate <bool CHEM_POT, bool RECIP>\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) chStencilKernel(\n    const double* __restrict__ in,    // c for the chemical potential, mu for the update\n    const double* __restrict__ cold,  // only used by the update sweep\n    double* __restrict__ out,\n    const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,\n    const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n    const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,\n    const double dtD, const int zChunk) {\n    __shared__ double s[BY + 2][BX + 2];\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BX + tx;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BY + ty;\n\n    // Threads outside the domain still participate in the loads (clamped) so\n    // that the halo entries they produce for their neighbours stay valid.\n    const int cx = (x < nx) ? x : nx - 1;\n    const int cy = (y < ny) ? y : ny - 1;\n    const bool active = (x < nx) && (y < ny);\n\n    const int xn = (cx > 0) ? cx - 1 : 0;\n    const int xp = (cx < nx - 1) ? cx + 1 : nx - 1;\n    const int yn = (cy > 0) ? cy - 1 : 0;\n    const int yp = (cy < ny - 1) ? cy + 1 : ny - 1;\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * static_cast<size_t>(ny);\n    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(cy) * nx;\n\n    const int z0 = blockIdx.z * zChunk;\n    const int z1 = min(z0 + zChunk, nz);\n    if (z0 >= z1) return;\n\n    const double dx2 = RECIP ? 1.0 / (dx * dx) : dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = RECIP ? 1.0 / (dy * dy) : dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = RECIP ? 1.0 / (dz * dz) : dz * dz;\n\n    double prev = in[static_cast<size_t>((z0 > 0) ? z0 - 1 : 0) * plane + row + cx];\n    double cur = in[static_cast<size_t>(z0) * plane + row + cx];\n\n    for (int z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n        const size_t zoff = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane;\n        const double next = in[static_cast<size_t>((z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : nz - 1) * plane + row + cx];\n\n        s[ty + 1][tx + 1] = cur;\n        if (tx == 0) s[ty + 1][0] = in[zoff + row + xn];\n        if (tx == BX - 1) s[ty + 1][BX + 1] = in[zoff + row + xp];\n        if (ty == 0) s[0][tx + 1] = in[zoff + static_cast<size_t>(yn) * nx + cx];\n        if (ty == BY - 1) s[BY + 1][tx + 1] = in[zoff + static_cast<size_t>(yp) * nx + cx];\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (active) {\n            const double sx = s[ty + 1][tx + 2] + s[ty + 1][tx] - 2.0 * cur;\n            const double sy = s[ty + 2][tx + 1] + s[ty][tx + 1] - 2.0 * cur;\n            const double sz = next + prev - 2.0 * cur;\n            const double cxx = RECIP ? sx * dx2 : sx / dx2;\n            const double cyy = RECIP ? sy * dy2 : sy / dy2;\n            const double czz = RECIP ? sz * dz2 : sz / dz2;\n            const double lap = cxx + cyy + czz;\n\n            const size_t idx = zoff + row + cx;\n            if (CHEM_POT) {\n                out[idx] = 4.5 * ((cur + 1.0) * e_AA + (cur - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cur * e_AB) + 3.0 * cur + cur * cur * cur - gamma * lap;\n            } else {\n                out[idx] = cold[idx] + dtD * lap;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        prev = cur;\n        cur = next;\n    }\n}\n\n// True if v != 0 and 1/v is exact, i.e. dividing by v and multiplying by 1/v\n// give bit-identical results (v is a power of two).\nbool reciprocalIsExact(const double v) {\n    if (!std::isnormal(v)) return false;\n    int exp = 0;\n    const double r = 1.0 / v;\n    return std::isnormal(r) && std::fabs(std::frexp(v, &exp)) == 0.5;\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n <bool CHEM_POT, bool RECIP>\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) chStencilKernel(\n    const double* __restrict__ in,    // c for the chemical potential, mu for the update\n    const double* min(z0 + zChunk, nz);\n    if (z0 >= z1) return;\n\n    const double dx2 = RECIP ? 1.0 / (dx * dx) : dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = RECIP ? 1.0 / (dy * dy) : dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = RECIP ? It is only enabled when 1/d^2 is exactly\n// representable (see reciprocalIsExact), in which case both forms are bit-identical\n// but the multiplication is far cheaper in double precision.\ntemplate <bool CHEM_POT, bool RECIP>\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) chStencilKernel(\n    const double* __restrict__ in,    // c for the chemical potential, mu for the update\n    const double* __restrict__ cold,  // only used by the update sweep\n    double* __restrict__ out,\n    const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,\n    const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n    const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,\n    const double dtD, const int zChunk) {\n    __shared__ double s[BY + 2][BX + 2];\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BX + tx;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BY + ty;\n\n    // Threads outside the domain still participate in the loads (clamped) so\n    // that the halo entries they produce for their neighbours stay valid.\n    const int cx = (x < nx) ? x : nx - 1;\n    const int cy = (y < ny) ? y : ny - 1;\n    const bool active = (x < nx) && (y < ny);\n\n    const int xn = (cx > 0) ? cx - 1 : 0;\n    const int xp = (cx < nx - 1) ? cx + 1 : nx - 1;\n    const int yn = (cy > 0) ? cy - 1 : 0;\n    const int yp = (cy < ny - 1) ? cy + 1 : ny - 1;\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * static_cast<size_t>(ny);\n    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(cy) * nx;\n\n    const int z0 = blockIdx.z * zChunk;\n    const int z1 = min(z0 + zChunk, nz);\n    if (z0 >= z1) return;\n\n    const double dx2 = RECIP ? 1.0 / (dx * dx) : dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = RECIP ? 1.0 / (dy * dy) : dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = RECIP ? All three forms produce bit-identical results for the spacings they\n// are selected for (see pickScaleMode), but double-precision division is by far\n// the most expensive instruction in the kernel, so it is avoided when possible.\nenum ScaleMode {\n    SCALE_DIV = 0,    // general case: divide by d^2\n    SCALE_RECIP = 1,  // d^2 is a power of two: multiply by the exact reciprocal\n    SCALE_UNIT = 2    // d^2 == 1: no scaling at all\n};\n\ntemplate <bool CHEM_POT, int SCALE_MODE>\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) chStencilKernel(\n    const double* __restrict__ in,    // c                          \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                      \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                            \\\n            printf(\"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_), __FILE__, __LINE__); \\\n            exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                                               \\\n        }                                                                                     \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// 3D index calculation\n__host__ __device__ inline size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Tile shape of the x-y plane processed by one thread block.\nstatic constexpr int BX = 32;\nstatic constexpr int BY = 4;\n\n// Both sweeps of the algorithm are 7-point stencils with clamped boundaries and\n// only differ in the per-cell arithmetic, so they share one templated kernel.\n// The kernel walks a chunk of z-planes: the x/y neighbours of the current plane\n// live in shared memory while the z neighbours are kept in registers and rolled\n// forward, so every cell is loaded from global memory essentially once.\n// SCALE_MODE picks how the second differences are divided by the squared grid\n// spacings. All three forms produce bit-identical results for the spacings they\n// are selected for (see pickScaleMode), but double-precision division is by far\n// the most expensive instruction in the kernel, so it is avoided when possible.\nenum ScaleMode {\n    SCALE_DIV = 0,    // general case: divide by d^2\n    SCALE_RECIP = 1,  // d^2 is a power of two: multiply by the exact reciprocal\n    SCALE_UNIT = 2    // d^2 == 1: no scaling at all\n};\n\ntemplate <bool CHEM_POT, int SCALE_MODE>\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) chStencilKernel(\n    const double* __restrict__ in,    // c for the chemical potential, mu for the update\n    const double* __restrict__ cold,  // only used by the update sweep\n    double* __restrict__ out,\n    const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,\n    const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n    const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,\n    const double dtD, const int zChunk) {\n    __shared__ double s[BY + 2][BX + 2];\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BX + tx;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BY + ty;\n\n    // Threads outside the domain still participate in the loads (clamped) so\n    // that the halo entries they produce for their neighbours stay valid.\n    const int cx = (x < nx) ? x : nx - 1;\n    const int cy = (y < ny) ? y : ny - 1;\n    const bool active = (x < nx) && (y < ny);\n\n    const int xn = (cx > 0) ? cx - 1 : 0;\n    const int xp = (cx < nx - 1) ? cx + 1 : nx - 1;\n    const int yn = (cy > 0) ? cy - 1 : 0;\n    const int yp = (cy < ny - 1) ? cy + 1 : ny - 1;\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * static_cast<size_t>(ny);\n    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(cy) * nx;\n\n    const int z0 = blockIdx.z * zChunk;\n    const int z1 = min(z0 + zChunk, nz);\n    if (z0 >= z1) return;\n\n    const double dx2 = (SCALE_MODE == SCALE_RECIP) ? 1.0 / (dx * dx) : dx * dx;\n    const xn];\n        if (tx == BX - 1) s[ty + 1][BX + 1] = in[zoff + row + xp];\n        if (ty == 0) s[0][tx + 1] = in[zoff + static_cast<size_t>(yn) * nx + cx];\n        if (ty == BY - 1) s[BY + 1][tx + 1] = in[zoff + static_cast<size_t>(yp) * nx + cx];\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (active) {\n            const double sx = s[ty + 1][tx + 2] + s[ty + 1][tx] - 2.0 * cur;\n            const double sy = s[ty + 2][tx + 1] + s[ty][tx + 1] - 2.0 * cur;\n            const double sz = next + prev - 2.0 * cur;\n            const double cxx = (SCALE_MODE == SCALE_UNIT) ? sx : ((SCALE_MODE == SCALE_RECIP) ? sx * dx2 : sx / dx2);\n            const double cyy = (SCALE_MODE == SCALE_UNIT) ? sy : ((SCALE_MODE == SCALE_RECIP) ? sy * dy2 : sy / dy2);\n            const double czz = (SCALE_MODE == SCALE_UNIT) ? sz : ((SCALE_MODE == SCALE_RECIP) ? sz * dz2 : sz / dz2);\n            const double lap = cxx + cyy + czz;\n\n            const size_t idx = zoff + row + cx;\n            if (CHEM_POT) {\n                out[idx] = 4.5 * ((cur + 1.0) * e_AA + (cur - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cur * e_AB) + 3.0 * cur + cur * cur * cur - gamma * lap;\n            } else {\n                out[idx] = cold[idx] + dtD * lap;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        prev = cur;\n        cur = next;\n    }\n}\n\n// Select the cheapest scaling variant that is bit-exact for the given squared\n// grid spacings: dividing by 1.0 is the identity, and dividing by a power of two\n// is exactly the same as multiplying by its (exactly representable) reciprocal.\nint pickScaleMode(const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2) {\n    auto isPowerOfTwo = [](const double v) {\n        int exp = 0;\n        return std::isnormal(v) && std::isnormal(1.0 / v) && std::fabs(std::frexp(v, &exp)) == 0.5;\n    };\n    if (dx2 == 1.0 && dy2 == 1.0 && dz2 == 1.0) return SCALE_UNIT;\n    if (isPowerOfTwo(dx2) && isPowerOfTwo(dy2) && isPowerOfTwo(dz2)) return SCALE_RECIP;\n    return SCALE_DIV;\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    //       maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > All three forms produce bit-identical results for the spacings they\n// are selected for (see pickScaleMode), but double-precision division is by far\n// the most expensive instruction in the kernel, so it is avoided when possible.\nenum ScaleMode {\n    SCALE_DIV = 0,    // general case: divide by d^2\n    SCALE_RECIP = 1,  // d^2 is a power of two: multiply by the exact reciprocal\n    SCALE_UNIT = 2    // d^2 == 1: no scaling at all\n};\n\ntemplate <bool CHEM_POT, int SCALE_MODE>\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) chStencilKernel(\n    const double* __restrict__ in,    // c for the chemical potential, mu for the update\n    const double* __restrict__ cold,  // only used by the update sweep\n    double* __restrict__ out,\n    const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,\n y : ny - 1;\n    const bool active = (x < nx) && (y < ny);\n\n    const int xn = (cx > 0) ? cx - 1 : 0;\n    const int xp = (cx < nx - 1) ? cx + 1 : nx - 1;\n    const int yn = (cy > 0) ? cy - 1 : 0;\n    const int yp = (cy < ny - 1) ? cy + 1 : ny - 1;\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * static_cast<size_t>(ny);\n    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(cy) * nx;\n\n    const int z0 = blockIdx.z * zChunk;\n    const int z1 = min(z0 + zChunk, nz);\n    if (z0 >= z1) return;\n\n    const double dx2 = (SCALE_MODE == SCALE_RECIP) ? 1.0 / (dx * dx) : dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = (SCALE_MODE == SCALE_RECIP) ? 1.0 / (dy * dy) : dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = (SCALE_MODE == SCALE_RECIP) ? 1.0 / (dz * dz) : dz * dz;\n\n    double prev = in[static_cast<size_t>((z0 > 0) ? z0 - 1 : 0) * plane + row + cx];\n    double cur = in[static_cast<size_t>(z0) * plane + row + cx];\n\n    for (int z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n        const size_t zoff = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane;\n        const double next = in[static_cast<size_t>((z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : nz - 1) * plane + row + cx];\n\n        s[ty + 1][tx + 1] = cur;\n        if (tx == 0) s[ty + 1][0] = in[zoff + row + xn];\n        if (tx == BX - 1) s[ty + 1][BX + 1] = in[zoff + row + xp];\n        if (ty == 0) s[0][tx + 1] = in[zoff + static_cast<size_t>(yn) * nx + cx];\n        if (ty == BY - 1) s[BY + 1][tx + 1] = in[zoff + static_cast<size_t>(yp) * nx + cx];\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (active) {\n            const double sx = s[ty + 1][tx + 2] + s[ty + 1][tx] - 2.0 * cur;\n            const double sy = s[ty + 2][tx + 1] + s[ty][tx + 1] - 2.0 * cur;\n            const double sz = next + prev - 2.0 * cur;\n            const double cxx = (SCALE_MODE == SCALE_UNIT) ? sx : ((SCALE_MODE == SCALE_RECIP) ? sx * dx2 : sx / dx2);\n            const double cyy = (SCALE_MODE == SCALE_UNIT) ? sy : ((SCALE_MODE == SCALE_RECIP) ? sy * dy2 : sy / dy2);\n            const double czz = (SCALE_MODE == SCALE_UNIT) ? sz : ((SCALE_MODE == SCALE_RECIP) ? sz * dz2 : sz / dz2);\n            const double lap = cxx + cyy + czz;\n\n            const size_t idx = zoff + row + cx;\n            if (CHEM_POT) {\n                out[idx] = 4.5 * ((cur + 1.0) * e_AA + (cur - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cur * e_AB) + 3.0 * cur + cur * cur * cur - gamma * lap;\n            } else {\n                out[idx] = cold[idx] + dtD * lap;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        prev = cur;\n        cur = next;\n    }\n}\n\n// Select the cheapest scaling variant that is bit-exact for the given squared\n// grid spacings: dividing by 1.0 is the identity, and dividing by a power of two\n// is exactly the same as multiplying by its (exactly representable) reciprocal.\nint pickScaleMode(const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2) {\n    auto isPowerOfTwo = [](const double v) {\n        int exp = 0;\n        return std::isnormal(v) && std::isnormal(1.0 / v) && std::fabs(std::frexp(v, &exp)) == 0.5;\n    };\n    if (dx2 == 1.0 && dy2 == 1.0 && dz2 == 1.0) return SCALE_UNIT;\n    if (isPowerOfTwo(dx2) && isPowerOfTwo(dy2) && isPowerOfTwo(dz2)) return SCALE_RECIP;\n    return SCALE_DIV;\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int         printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: All three forms produce bit-identical results for the spacings they\n// are selected for (see pickScaleMode), but double-precision division is by far\n// the most expensive instruction in the kernel, so it is avoided when possible.\nenum ScaleMode {\n    SCALE_DIV = 0,    // general case: divide by d^2\n    SCALE_RECIP = 1,  // d^2 is a power of two: multiply by the exact reciprocal\n    SCALE_UNIT = 2    // d^2 == 1: no scaling at all\n};\n\ntemplate <bool CHEM_POT, int SCALE_MODE>\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) chStencilKernel(\n    const double* __restrict__ in,    // c for the chemical potential, mu for the update\n    const double* __restrict__ cold,  // only used by the update sweep\n    double* __restrict__ out,\n    const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,\n    const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n    const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,\n    const double dtD, const int zChunk) {\n    __shared__ double s[BY + 2][BX + 2];\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BX + tx;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BY + ty;\n\n    // Threads outside the domain still participate in the loads (clamped) so\n    // that the halo entries they produce for their neighbours stay valid.\n    const int cx = (x < nx) ? x : nx - 1;\n    const int cy = (y < ny) ? y : ny - 1;\n    const bool active = (x < nx) && (y < ny);\n\n    const int xn = (cx > 0) ? cx - 1 : 0;\n    const int xp = (cx < nx - 1) ? cx + 1 : nx - 1;\n    const int yn = (cy > 0) ? cy - 1 : 0;\n    const int yp = (cy < ny - 1) ? cy + 1 : ny - 1;\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * static_cast<size_t>(ny);\n    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(cy) * nx;\n\n    const int z0 = blockIdx.z * zChunk;\n    const int z1 = min(z0 + zChunk, nz);\n    if (z0 >= z1) return;\n\n    const double dx2 = (SCALE_MODE == SCALE_RECIP) ? 1.0 / (dx * dx) : dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = (SCALE_MODE == SCALE_RECIP) ? 1.0 / (dy * dy) : dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = (SCALE_MODE == SCALE_RECIP) ? 1.0 / (dz * dz) : dz * dz;\n\n    double prev = in[static_cast<size_t>((z0 > 0) ? z0 - 1 : 0) * plane + row + cx];\n    double cur = in[static_cast<size_t>(z0) * plane + row + cx];\n\n    for (int z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n        const size_t zoff = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane;\n        const double next = in[static_cast<size_t>((z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : nz - 1) * plane + row + cx];\n\n        s[ty + 1][tx + 1] = cur;\n        if (tx == 0) s[ty + 1][0] = in[zoff + row + xn];\n        if (tx == BX - 1) s[ty + 1][BX + 1] = in[zoff + row + xp];\n        if (ty == 0) s[0][tx + 1] = in[zoff + static_cast<size_t>(yn) * nx + cx];\n        if (ty == BY - 1) s[BY + 1][tx + 1] = in[zoff + static_cast<size_t>(yp) * nx + cx];\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (active) {\n            const double sx = s[ty + 1][tx + 2] + s[ty + 1][tx] - 2.0 * cur;\n            const (CHEM_POT) {\n                // cur * (2 * e_AB) is bit-identical to (2 * cur) * e_AB (scaling by\n                // two is exact) and hoists the doubling out of the inner loop.\n                out[idx] = 4.5 * ((cur + 1.0) * e_AA + (cur - 1.0) * e_BB - cur * (2.0 * e_AB)) + 3.0 * cur + cur * cur * cur - gamma * lap;\n            } else {\n                out[idx] = cold[idx] + dtD * lap;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        prev = cur;\n        cur = next;\n    }\n}\n\n// Select the cheapest scaling variant that is bit-exact for the given squared\n// grid double dy2, const double dz2) {\n    auto isPowerOfTwo = [](const double v) {\n        int exp = 0;\n        return std::isnormal(v) && std::isnormal(1.0 / v) && std::fabs(std::frexp(v, &exp)) == 0.5;\n    };\n    if (dx2 == 1.0 && dy2 == 1.0 && dz2 == 1.0) return SCALE_UNIT;\n    if (isPowerOfTwo(dx2) && isPowerOfTwo(dy2) && isPowerOfTwo(dz2)) return SCALE_RECIP;\n    return SCALE_DIV;\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After double dy2, const double dz2) {\n    auto isPowerOfTwo = [](const double v) {\n        int exp = 0;\n        return std::isnormal(v) && std::isnormal(1.0 / v) && std::fabs(std::frexp(v, &exp)) == 0.5;\n    };\n    if (dx2 == 1.0 && dy2 == 1.0 && dz2 == 1.0) return SCALE_UNIT;\n    if (isPowerOfTwo(dx2) && isPowerOfTwo(dy2) && isPowerOfTwo(dz2)) return SCALE_RECIP;\n    return SCALE_DIV;\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int         printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    printf(\"Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation  const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double gamma = 0.5;\n    const double D = 1.0;\n\n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    // Allocate arrays\n BY - 1) / BY);\n    const int targetBlocks = prop.multiProcessorCount * 32;\n    int zBlocks = (targetBlocks + blocksXY - 1) / blocksXY;\n    zBlocks = std::min(std::max(zBlocks, 1), inz);\n    const the concentration\n        launchStep(grid, zChunk);\n\n        // Swap buffers\n        std::swap(d_cold, d_cnew);\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_cnew));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_mu));\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(cold, \"Concentration\");\n    }\n\n \\\n            exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                                               \\\n        }                                                                                     \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// 3D index calculation\n__host__ __device__ inline size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Tile shape of the x-y plane processed by one thread block.\nstatic constexpr int BX = 32;\nstatic constexpr int BY = 4;\n\n// Both sweeps of the algorithm are 7-point stencils with clamped boundaries and\n// only differ in the per-cell arithmetic, so they share one templated kernel.\n// The kernel walks a chunk of z-planes: the x/y neighbours of the current plane\n// live in shared memory while the z neighbours are kept in registers and rolled\n// forward, so every cell is loaded from global memory essentially once.\n// SCALE_MODE picks how the second differences are divided by the squared grid\n// spacings. All three forms produce bit-identical results for the spacings they\n// are selected for (see pickScaleMode), but double-precision division is by far\n// the most expensive instruction in the kernel, so it is avoided when possible.\nenum ScaleMode {\n    SCALE_DIV = 0,    // general case: divide by d^2\n    SCALE_RECIP = 1,  // d^2 is a power of two: multiply by the exact reciprocal\n    SCALE_UNIT = 2    // d^2 == 1: no scaling at all\n};\n\ntemplate <bool CHEM_POT, int SCALE_MODE>\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) chStencilKernel(\n    const double* __restrict__ in,    // c for the chemical potential, mu for the update\n    const double* __restrict__ cold,  // only used by the update sweep\n    double* __restrict__ out,\n    const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,\n    const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n    const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,\n    const double dtD, const int zChunk) {\n    __shared__ double s[BY + 2][BX + 2];\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BX + tx;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BY + ty;\n\n    // Threads outside the domain still participate in the loads (clamped) so\n    // that the halo entries they produce for their neighbours stay valid.\n    const int cx = (x < nx) ? x : nx - 1;\n    const int cy = (y < ny) ? y : ny - 1;\n    const bool active = (x < nx) && (y < ny);\n\n    const int xn = (cx > 0) ? cx - 1 : 0;\n    const int xp = (cx < nx - 1) ? cx + 1 : nx - 1;\n    const int yn = (cy > 0) ? cy - 1 : 0;\n    const int yp = (cy < ny - 1) ? cy + 1 : ny - 1;\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * static_cast<size_t>(ny);\n    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(cy) * nx;\n\n    const int z0 = blockIdx.z * zChunk;\n    const int z1 = min(z0 + zChunk, nz);\n    if (z0 >= z1) return;\n\n    const double dx2 = (SCALE_MODE == SCALE_RECIP) ? 1.0 / (dx * dx) : dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = (SCALE_MODE == SCALE_RECIP) ? 1.0 / (dy * dy) : dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = (SCALE_MODE == SCALE_RECIP) ? 1.0 / (dz * dz) : dz * dz;\n\n    double prev = in[static_cast<size_t>((z0 > 0) ? z0 - 1 : 0) * plane + row + cx];\n    double cur = in[static_cast<size_t>(z0) * plane + row + cx];\n\n    for (int z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n        const size_t zoff = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane;\n        const double next = in[static_cast<size_t>((z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : nz - 1) * plane + row + cx];\n\n        s[ty + 1][tx + 1] = cur;\n        if (tx == 0) s[ty + 1][0] = in[zoff + row + xn];\n        if (tx == BX - 1) s[ty + 1][BX + 1] = in[zoff + row + xp];\n        if (ty == 0) s[0][tx + 1] = in[zoff + static_cast<size_t>(yn) * nx + cx];\n        if (ty == BY - 1) s[BY + 1][tx + 1] = in[zoff + static_cast<size_t>(yp) * nx + cx];\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (active) {\n            const double sx = s[ty + 1][tx + 2] + s[ty + 1][tx] - 2.0 * cur;\n            const double sy = s[ty + 2][tx + 1] + s[ty][tx + 1] - 2.0 * cur;\n            const double sz = next + prev - 2.0 * cur;\n            const double cxx = (SCALE_MODE == SCALE_UNIT) ? sx : ((SCALE_MODE == SCALE_RECIP) ? sx * dx2 : sx / dx2);\n            const double cyy = (SCALE_MODE == SCALE_UNIT) ? sy : ((SCALE_MODE == SCALE_RECIP) ? sy * dy2 : sy / dy2);\n            const double czz = (SCALE_MODE == SCALE_UNIT) ? sz : ((SCALE_MODE == SCALE_RECIP) ? sz * dz2 : sz / dz2);\n            const double lap = cxx + cyy + czz;\n\n            const size_t idx = zoff + row + cx;\n            if (CHEM_POT) {\n                // cur * (2 * e_AB) is bit-identical to (2 * cur) * e_AB (scaling by\n                // two is exact) and hoists the doubling out of the inner loop.\n                out[idx] = 4.5 * ((cur + 1.0) * e_AA + (cur - 1.0) * e_BB - cur * (2.0 * e_AB)) + 3.0 * cur + cur * cur * cur - gamma * lap;\n            } else {\n                out[idx] = cold[idx] + dtD * lap;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        prev = cur;\n        cur = next;\n    }\n}\n\n// Select the cheapest scaling variant that is bit-exact for the given squared\n// grid spacings: dividing by 1.0 is the identity, and dividing by a power of two\n// is exactly the same as multiplying by its (exactly representable) reciprocal.\nint pickScaleMode(const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2) {\n    auto isPowerOfTwo = [](const double v) {\n        int exp = 0;\n        return std::isnormal(v) && std::isnormal(1.0 / v) && std::fabs(std::frexp(v, &exp)) == 0.5;\n    };\n    if (dx2 == 1.0 && dy2 == 1.0 && dz2 == 1.0) return SCALE_UNIT;\n    if (isPowerOfTwo(dx2) && isPowerOfTwo(dy2) && isPowerOfTwo(dz2)) return SCALE_RECIP;\n    return SCALE_DIV;\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0         printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n    printf(\"Time steps: %d\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Physical parameters\n    const double dx = 1.0;\n    const double dy = 1.0;\n    const double dz = 1.0;\n    const double dt = 0.01;\n    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double gamma = 0.5;\n    const double D = 1.0;\n\n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    // Allocate arrays\n    std::vector<double> cold(gridSize);\n\n    // Initialize concentration field\n    printf(\"Initializing concentration field...\\n\");\n    initializeConcentration(cold, nx, ny, nz);\n\n    // Set up prop.name, prop.multiProcessorCount);\n\n    double *d_cold = nullptr, *d_cnew = nullptr, *d_mu = nullptr;\n    const size_t bytes = gridSize * sizeof(double);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_cold, std::max<size_t>(bytes, 1)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_cnew, std::max<size_t>(bytes, 1)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_mu, std::max<size_t>(bytes, 1)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_cold, sweeps a contiguous range of z-planes, which lets it reuse the\n    // z neighbours it has already loaded. The range is kept short enough that\n    // there are plenty of blocks to fill all SMs, and capped so that even very\n    // deep grids keep enough blocks in flight to hide memory latency.\n    const int blocksXY = std::max(((inx + BX - 1) / BX) * ((iny + BY - 1) / BY), 1);\n    const int targetBlocks = prop.multiProcessorCount * 32;\n    const int zBlocks = std::max(std::min((targetBlocks + blocksXY - 1) / blocksXY, inz), 1);\n    const int zChunk = std::min(std::max((inz + zBlocks - 1) / zBlocks, 1), 16);\n\n    const dim3 block(BX, BY, 1);\n    const dim3 grid(std::max((inx + BX - 1) / BX, 1), std::max((iny + BY - 1) / BY, 1),\n                    std::max((inz + zChunk - 1) / zChunk, 1));\n\n    const double dtD = dt * D;\n    const int scaleMode = pickScaleMode(dx * dx, dy * dy, dz * dz);\n\n    // One time step: chemical potential from c, then the concentration update from mu.\n    auto launchStep = [&](const dim3& g, const int chunk) {\n        auto launch = [&](auto mode) {\n            constexpr int M = decltype(mode)::value;\n            chStencilKernel<true, M><<<g, block>>>(d_cold, nullptr, d_mu, inx, iny, inz,\n                                                   dx, dy, dz, gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB, dtD, chunk);\n            chStencilKernel<false, M><<<g, block>>>(d_mu, d_cold, d_cnew, inx, iny, inz,\n                                                    dx, dy, dz, gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB, dtD, chunk);\n        };\n        switch (scaleMode) {\n            case SCALE_UNIT: launch(std::integral_constant<int, SCALE_UNIT>{}); break;\n            case SCALE_RECIP: launch(std::integral_constant<int, SCALE_RECIP>{}); break;\n            default: launch(std::integral_constant<int, SCALE_DIV>{}); break;\n        }\n    };\n\n    // Force module load / kernel setup up front so it does not pollute the timing.\n    // zChunk == 0 makes every block exit immediately without touching the data.\n    launchStep(dim3(1, 1, 1), 0);\n\n    // Run simulation\n    printf(\"Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\\n\");\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {\n        // Compute chemical potential, then update the concentration\n        launchStep(grid, zChunk);\n\n        // Swap buffers\n        std::swap(d_cold, d_cnew);\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate performance\n    double cellUpdates = (double)gridSize * iterations;\n    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(cold.data(), d_cold, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_cold));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_cnew));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_mu));\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(cold, \"Concentration\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateResult(cold, nx, ny, nz);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":324,"oldLines":12,"newStart":324,"newLines":15,"lines":[" ","     auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();","     auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);","+    // Microsecond resolution keeps the rate meaningful for runs that take well","+    // under a millisecond on the GPU; {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011Ceh8wyXbevVoxMVwx43TZ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. The benchmark now runs entirely on the GPU with CUDA.\n\n## What changed\n\n**`cahn-hilliard/cahn_hilliard.cpp`**\n- Both sweeps (chemical potential, concentration update) are 7-point stencils with clamped boundaries, so they share one templated kernel `chStencilKernel<CHEM_POT, SCALE_MODE>`. Each thread block owns a 32×4 tile in x-y and sweeps a range of z-planes: the x/y neighbours go through shared memory (with clamped halo loads), while the z neighbours are kept in registers and rolled forward, so each cell is read from global memory essentially once.\n- Grid decomposition is tuned at runtime from the SM count: enough z-chunks to fill all SMs, capped at 16 planes per block so deep grids keep enough blocks in flight.\n- `SCALE_MODE` picks how the second differences are divided by `d²`: identity when `d²==1`, multiply-by-reciprocal when `d²` is a power of two, plain division otherwise. All three are bit-identical for the spacings they're selected for; removing FP64 division was the single largest win (~2.2×), since FP64 divides are extremely slow on consumer GPUs.\n- Buffers live on the device for the whole run; only one H2D copy before and one D2H copy after the timed loop. A dummy launch before the timer forces module load out of the measurement.\n- Host-side code (init, validation, `print_results`, CLI) is unchanged. The MCUPS figure is now computed from a microsecond-resolution duration, since GPU runs of the default size finish in well under 1 ms and would otherwise print `inf`; the printed \"Computation time: N ms\" line is unchanged.\n\n**`CMakeLists.txt`** — enables the CUDA language, compiles `cahn_hilliard.cpp` as CUDA (file name and executable name kept), targets `native` architecture with a fallback list for CMake < 3.24.\n\n## Results (RTX 3090, one GPU)\n\n| grid | CPU baseline | CUDA | speedup |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| 64³ | 169 MCUPS | 6530 | 39× |\n| 256³ | 168 | 7997 | 48× |\n| 512³ | — | 8946 | ~53× |\n\nCorrectness: compared against the original CPU build on 8 configurations (cubic, rectangular, non-multiples of the tile size, `nx=1`, `nz=1`); sum/min/max/samples agree to ≤3.5e-13 relative. The residual difference is FMA contraction only — building with `-fmad=false` changes the GPU hash, and the CPU baseline itself contracts under `-march=native`, so no bitwise match is attainable in either direction; I kept FMA on since it is ~20% faster.\n\nTwo notes on scope: profiling shows the update sweep at ~620 GB/s (near DRAM roofline) and the potential sweep pinned at the FP64 instruction roofline, so further single-GPU gains would require algebraically refactoring the chemical-potential polynomial — I left it in the original evaluation order rather than trade numerics for ~25%. I also kept this single-GPU; the machine has 4× RTX 3090, and a z-decomposition with halo exchange could get roughly 3–3.5× more on large grids, but it would slow the default 64³ case and adds correctness risk, so say the word if you want it."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":554,"cache_read_input_tokens":95453,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":554},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e01a2b29-4555-4b41-8311-bfe5b6ee5f51","uuid":"8ab5b1a1-9a73-431d-b943-608040c85e71","timestamp":"2026-09-03T20:29:14.453Z","request_id":"req_011Ceh8wxw8SpkcHu6EFJepk"}
